- **feature-builder**: Implements a feature end to end in this codebase (pages, server actions, components, schema) following the project conventions. - **feature-builder**: Implements product features in small, safe, shippable steps. Use this agent when you know what needs to be built. ([SmaRTy-Saini/founder-agent-os](https://github.com/SmaRTy-Saini/founder-agent-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-builder.md))

- **Feature-Builder**: Use when a new feature or capability needs end-to-end implementation from requirements, including scaffolding, logic and docstrings; not for executing a pre-written TODO list (use Implementer) or fixing bugs. - **feature-builder**: You implement backend feature phases end-to-end. ([tarekBassiouny/najaah-backend](https://github.com/tarekBassiouny/najaah-backend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-builder.md))
- **feature-builder**: Use this agent when you need to implement new features in Rust, Python, or Java projects. This includes designing and building new modules, APIs, data pipelines, services, or CLI tools from requirements — following idiomatic patterns and the project's established architecture. ([thenomadlad/dotfiles](https://github.com/thenomadlad/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-builder.md))
- **feature-builder**: Use when implementing a feature, fixing a bug, or changing behaviour in this repo. Drives the work in order and enforces the definition of done. Delegate to it rather than editing ad hoc when a change touches more than one file. ([timogee/appointment-id](https://github.com/timogee/appointment-id/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-builder.md))
- **feature-builder**: Implements a feature from a GitHub issue in an app built on the agentic-kit architecture. Reads the issue, explores the relevant code, writes the implementation following the kit's architecture and Effect rules, opens a PR with small layer-aligned commits, and reports what it changed. Use to implement or fix a feature described in a GitHub issue. ([timothyrusso/agentic-kit](https://github.com/timothyrusso/agentic-kit/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/feature-builder.md))
- **feature-builder**: Implements a new entity or CRUD feature end to end (contract, mock, query layer, form dialog, list and details pages, navigation, tests) by following the add-feature skill, then runs the verify gates. Use for work that spans several layers; for a small edit, work directly. ([tumarok/coworking-admin-next](https://github.com/tumarok/coworking-admin-next/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-builder.md))

- **feature-completeness-auditor**: Audits a new or changed feature for the "looks done but isn't wired" class of gaps — affordances that appear finished but trace to nothing real (dead controls, broken-promise copy, settings that don't persist, demo data on a live surface, empty lists, totals that don't match their source). Invoke AFTER building and BEFORE deploy, for any change that adds or alters a user-facing affordance (button, form, toggle, link, metric, list, setting) or a backend module. - **features**: &gt; Технічний довідник можливостей: команди, джерела, заміри. Для помічника й &gt; для того, хто хоче знати подробиці. Людині простими словами — &gt; [«Як працювати»](../start.md). ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/features.md) · ★20)
- **features**: This catalog connects user capabilities to their implementation and build gates. [`Cargo.toml`](../../Cargo.toml) is authoritative for feature composition; [`src/main.rs`](../../src/main.rs), [`src/cli/`](../../src/cli/), and [`src/mcp/mode.rs`](../../src/mcp/mode.rs) define the public vocabulary. Consult CLI `--help` for exact flags rather than inferring them from MCP fields. ([jamon8888/basemind](https://github.com/jamon8888/basemind/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/features.md))
- **FEATURES**: Where each feature lives and how it works, so you can open the right files without searching. ([MartinSvanholm/TheMealWorkshop](https://github.com/MartinSvanholm/TheMealWorkshop/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/FEATURES.md))
- **features**: - **Authentication**: Email/password + OAuth (Discord, Steam) via Better Auth - **Organizations**: Multi-user orgs with permission levels (Owner, Admin, Viewer) - **Projects**: Create and configure projects, receive API key pairs (publishable + secret) - **Event Ingestion API**: HTTP POST `/v1/events` with batch support - **Spatial Read API**: HTTP GET `/v1/spatial/{voxels,scenes,heatmap,trajector ([Noot-Studio/analytics](https://github.com/Noot-Studio/analytics/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/features.md))
- **features**: Quality and architectural improvements, prioritized by impact. A heading ends with its status (SHIPPED, PARTIAL, OPEN, or CLOSED for an entry other changes overtook) once the entry has been checked against the code; file and line references are from when an entry was written and drift as the files change. ([seriouslysean/wordbun](https://github.com/seriouslysean/wordbun/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/features.md))
- **features-auditor**: This agent should be used when auditing src/features/ directory for dead code, unused exports, and duplication. Triggers on: "audit features", "find dead code in features", "clean up features/", "check features for unused code". &lt;example&gt; Context: User wants to audit feature modules for cleanup user: "Проверь features на мёртвый код" assistant: "Запускаю features-auditor для анализа неиспользуемых экспортов и дубликатов" &lt;/example&gt; ([izmailovilya/ilia-izmailov-plugins](https://github.com/izmailovilya/ilia-izmailov-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/vibe-audit/agents/features-auditor.md) · ★46)
- **features-auditor**: This agent should be used when auditing src/features/ directory for dead code, unused exports, and duplication. - **features-keeper**: Maintainer only (needs pmctl with the maintainer's tracker DB, project polymux). Maintains FEATURES.md, the user-facing feature catalogue of pm. After a feature lands it adds or updates the entry; on request it audits the whole app for features that are missing, stale or removed and fixes the file. Changes FEATURES.md only through `pmctl feature` (and CHANGELOG through `pmctl change add` when asked). Contributors do not use it: they describe the change in the PR instead. ([dungtq/polymux](https://github.com/dungtq/polymux/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/features-keeper.md) · ★3)

- **fec-architect**: 当任务涉及页面拆分、组件架构、状态流设计、目录规划、数据流设计、模块边界划分或大型前端重构时，使用该子代理。将架构方案报告保存为 Markdown 文件。 ([bovinphang/frontend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/frontend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/agents/fec-architect.md) · ★24)

- **fec-code-reviewer**: 专注于前端代码（React/Vue/Next/Nuxt、TypeScript、样式、客户端安全）的资深评审。在编写或修改前端后委托；默认只输出并落盘评审报告，不直接修改业务代码。按 CRITICAL→LOW 检查、控制噪声并合并同类问题，报告写入 reports。适合结合 git diff 的独立 Code Review。 ([bovinphang/frontend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/frontend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/agents/fec-code-reviewer.md) · ★24)
- **fec-code-reviewer**: Senior review focusing on front-end code (React/Vue/Next/Nuxt, TypeScript, styles, client-side security). Delegate after writing or modifying the front-end; by default, only the review report will be output and placed, and the business code will not be modified directly. Press CRITICAL→LOW to check, control noise and merge similar problems, and write the report into reports. Suitable for independent code review combined with git diff. ([bovinphang/frontend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/frontend-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/fec-code-reviewer.md) · ★24)
- **fec-debugger**: Front-end diagnostic and repair subagent: Handle build failures, runtime errors, UI exceptions, and interface issues using a unified 5-step diagnostic framework. - **fec-debugger**: 前端诊断与修复子代理：使用统一 5 步诊断框架处理构建失败、运行时错误、UI 异常、接口问题。适合复杂或多层嵌套的前端问题排查。 ([bovinphang/frontend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/frontend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/agents/fec-debugger.md) · ★24)

- **fec-e2e-runner**: 前端端到端测试专精子代理：编写与维护关键用户旅程、执行 Playwright/Cypress、治理不稳定用例、管理截图/Trace/视频与 CI 产物。在需要生成、运行或修复 E2E、或保障核心流程可测时委托。若环境已安装 Vercel Agent Browser 等语义化浏览器工具可优先使用，否则以 Playwright 为主。 ([bovinphang/frontend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/frontend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/agents/fec-e2e-runner.md) · ★24)

- **fec-performance-optimizer**: 前端性能分析与优化专精：Core Web Vitals、打包体积、运行时与渲染、网络与缓存、内存泄漏排查；可配合 Lighthouse、Bundle 分析与 Profiler。当用户提到页面慢、卡顿、首屏、包体积、渲染差、Web Vitals 不达标时使用。 ([bovinphang/frontend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/frontend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/agents/fec-performance-optimizer.md) · ★24)
- **fec-performance-optimizer**: Front-end performance analysis and optimization specialization: Core Web Vitals, packaging volume, runtime and rendering, network and cache, memory leak troubleshooting; can cooperate with Lighthouse, Bundle analysis and Profiler. - **fec-researcher**: Deep campaign-finance investigator for U.S. federal politics, powered by the OpenFEC MCP tools. - **fec-ui-checker**: 使用该子代理排查前端 UI 中的视觉缺陷、布局错乱、CSS 问题、响应式异常以及交互与设计不一致的问题，将报告保存为 Markdown 文件。支持从 Figma、Sketch、MasterGo、Pixso、墨刀、摹客获取设计数据，对比设计稿与实现结果，评估还原度并给出具体差异报告。 ([bovinphang/frontend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/frontend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/agents/fec-ui-checker.md) · ★24)
